Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The radioactivity operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 101. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 406 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 101 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the radioactivity structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 406. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
